theorem cos_pi_div_seven_sub_cos_two_pi_div_seven_add_cos_three_pi_div_seven : Real.cos (Real.pi / 7) - Real.cos (2 * Real.pi / 7) + Real.cos (3 * Real.pi / 7) = 1 / 2 := by sorry
theorem unique_solution_iff (c : ℝ) (f : ℝ → ℝ) (hf : ∀ x, f x = |x - 20| + |x + 18|) : (∃! x : ℝ, f x = x + c) ↔ c = 18 := by sorry
theorem sum_sq_ineq (n : ℕ) (hn : 0 < n) (a : ℕ → ℝ) (ha : ∀ i, 1 ≤ i → i ≤ n → 0 < a i) (A : ℕ → ℝ) (hA : ∀ k, 1 ≤ k → k ≤ n → A k = (∑ i ∈ Finset.Icc 1 k, a i) / k) : (A n - 1 / A n) ^ 2 ≤ (1 / n) * ∑ i ∈ Finset.Icc 1 n, (a i - 1 / a i) ^ 2 := by sorry
theorem sqrt_sq_add_sqrt_sq_of_lt_lt_zero (x y : ℝ) (hxy : x < y) (hy : y < 0) : Real.sqrt ((x - y) ^ 2) + Real.sqrt ((x + y) ^ 2) = -2 * x := by sorry
theorem x_seven_add_64_mul_x_sq (x : ℝ) (hx : x ^ 3 + 4 * x = 8) : x ^ 7 + 64 * x ^ 2 = 128 := by sorry
theorem find_w (x y z w : ℝ) (h1 : 2*x + y + z + w = 1) (h2 : x + 3*y + z + w = 2) (h3 : x + y + 4*z + w = 3) (h4 : x + y + z + 5*w = 25) : w = 11/2 := by sorry
theorem gcd_twelve_n_plus_five_nine_n_plus_four (n : ℤ) : Int.gcd (12 * n + 5) (9 * n + 4) = 1 := by sorry
theorem add_div_ge (n : ℕ) (hn : 0 < n) (x : ℝ) (hx : x ≥ (n : ℝ)) : x + (n : ℝ) / x ≥ (n : ℝ) + 1 ∧ (x + (n : ℝ) / x = (n : ℝ) + 1 ↔ x = (n : ℝ)) := by sorry
theorem prime_triple_sum_of_products (p q r : ℕ) (k : ℕ) (hp : Nat.Prime p) (hq : Nat.Prime q) (hr : Nat.Prime r) (h : p * q + q * r + r * p = 12 * k + 1) : k = 5 ∧ ((p, q, r) = (2, 3, 11) ∨ (p, q, r) = (2, 11, 3) ∨ (p, q, r) = (3, 11, 2) ∨ (p, q, r) = (3, 2, 11) ∨ (p, q, r) = (11, 2, 3) ∨ (p, q, r) = (11, 3, 2)) := by sorry
theorem least_value_eq_twelve : IsLeast {y : ℝ | ∃ x : ℝ, 0 < x ∧ x < π ∧ y = (9 * x ^ 2 * Real.sin x ^ 2 + 4) / (x * Real.sin x)} 12 := by sorry
theorem sin_cos_ineq (x y z : ℝ) (hx : 0 < x) (hx' : x < Real.pi / 2) (hy : 0 < y) (hy' : y < Real.pi / 2) (hz : 0 < z) (hz' : z < Real.pi / 2) : Real.pi / 2 + 2 * Real.sin x * Real.cos y + 2 * Real.sin y * Real.cos z > Real.sin (2 * x) + Real.sin (2 * y) + Real.sin (2 * z) := by sorry
theorem sin_cos_ineq (x : Fin 10 → ℝ) (hx : ∀ i, x i ∈ Set.Icc 0 (Real.pi / 2)) (hsum : ∑ i, (Real.sin (x i))^2 = 1) : 3 * (∑ i, Real.sin (x i)) ≤ ∑ i, Real.cos (x i) := by sorry
theorem solution_set (x : ℝ) : -4 * (x - 5) * (x + 4) * (x - 1) ≤ 0 ↔ x ∈ Set.Icc (-4 : ℝ) 1 ∪ Set.Ici (5 : ℝ) := by sorry
theorem sum_triple_products_le (a b c d : ℝ) (ha : 0 ≤ a) (hb : 0 ≤ b) (hc : 0 ≤ c) (hd : 0 ≤ d) (habcd : a + b + c + d = 1) : b * c * d + c * d * a + d * a * b + a * b * c ≤ 1 / 27 + (176 / 27) * (a * b * c * d) := by sorry
theorem count_pairs : Set.ncard {p : ℤ × ℤ | p.1 ^ 2 + p.2 ^ 2 < 16 ∧ p.1 ^ 2 + p.2 ^ 2 < 8 * p.1 ∧ p.1 ^ 2 + p.2 ^ 2 < 8 * p.2} = 6 := by sorry
theorem exactly_one_real_root_iff (θ : ℝ) (hθ : Real.cos θ ≠ 0) (P : ℝ → ℝ) (hP : ∀ x : ℝ, P x = Real.sin θ * x ^ 2 + (Real.cos θ + Real.tan θ) * x + 1) : (∃! x : ℝ, P x = 0) ↔ (Real.sin θ = 0 ∨ Real.sin θ = (Real.sqrt 5 - 1) / 2) := by sorry
theorem quadruples (x y z t : ℕ) (hx : 0 < x) (hy : 0 < y) (hz : 0 < z) (ht : 0 < t) : 20 ^ x + 14 ^ (2 * y) = (x + 2 * y + z) ^ (z * t) ↔ (x, y, z, t) = (1, 1, 3, 1) := by sorry
theorem exists_partial_sum_bound (n : ℕ) (a : ℕ → ℝ) : ∃ k ∈ Finset.range (n + 1), ∀ x : ℝ, x ∈ Set.Icc (0 : ℝ) 1 → ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (n + 1), a i * x ^ i ≤ ∑ i ∈ Finset.range (k + 1), a i := by sorry
theorem greatest_prime_factor_binom_200_100 : IsGreatest {p : ℕ | p.Prime ∧ p ∣ Nat.choose 200 100 ∧ p < 100} 61 := by sorry
theorem min_value_sqrt : IsLeast {y : ℝ | ∃ x : ℝ, -1 ≤ x ∧ x ≤ 1 ∧ y = Real.sqrt (58 - 42 * x) + Real.sqrt (149 - 140 * Real.sqrt (1 - x ^ 2))} (Real.sqrt 109) := by sorry
